HEADERS: every .py/.sh/.md/.conf file's title reads v5.0 with a `v5.0  2026-10-25  OTV5 r7` entry above the inherited changelog (515 files), and 17 files that had no title version gained one. SIX files had already spent v5.0 in their own history (levels, position_manager, gex_pin_butterfly, runaway_continuation, sweep_credit_spread, trend_credit_spread) and land at the next free v5 decimal (v5.7, v5.23, v5.12, v5.5, v5.5, v5.2), said in their own reset line - the gate refuses a version string that already existed at HEAD, and a history line is never rewritten. KEPT, DELIBERATELY: every `OTV4TEST rN` / `QQQ-TEST` citation in docstrings, PLAN_SPEC, TRADES and this backlog's PART 1 - the prefix IS the boundary marker, and rewriting ~1,800 history lines would sever them from the predecessor's public ledger. The trade lineage stamp (`TEST`) is r8, with its control half. FULL SWEEP on the staged tree: 286 of 287 checkers green (check_wake_box is gone); the one red is check_emergency_watchdog W16, environmental on control and green on a box. | ✅ CLOSED r7 |
| **BOOT.9** | ✅ **CLOSED r6** — THE DAILY BRIEF AND THE BOX AGENT'S SETTINGS STILL DESCRIBED A BOX THAT CONTROLS ITS OWN REPO AND ACTS ON ITSELF. `docs/HANDOFF.md` v2.2 was the predecessor fork's QQQ-TEST brief: QQQ-TEST as the repo's controller, "no test box pushes" (an OTV5 managed box pushes by design), a Saturday research run on a harness no OTV5 box has, `GENESIS-TEST.md`, and a grant to fix what is yours (restart the feed). `deploy/claude-user-settings-pull-only.json` pre-allowed stopping, starting and restarting optionsbot and candle-feed, daemon-reload, `git pull`, edits to worktrees and any tool under the bot's broker credentials (`run_with_bot_env.py`). The operator, 2026-10-25: *"We will need an accurate handoff brief that differs from the 1st boot orientation brief"*; *"a trading box under your control will not be making repo decisions or trading decisions the way QQQ-Test currently enjoys"*. HANDOFF v3.0, written fresh: where we are (paper until SOFI cash 2026-11-23, positive EV over the period, fees outside EV), BOX IDENTITY, WHO OWNS WHAT (1-REPORTER the custodian; every code fix through it; NO TRADING DECISIONS; what the agent does alone: read, read-only runs into scratch, the boot routine, the observe-only loss verdict, report), WA §38.2's grants to act read as "report it", the push as the operator's switch, the boot order the operator ruled with the awake line to 1-REPORTER, URGENT pages reported with a proposed action and no action taken. Settings: those grants removed and DENIED, with configure.sh, `git pull/reset/checkout`, the primer's and seeder's `--apply`, data_capture switching and every remaining "ask" item; a NO TRADING DECISIONS hard deny; the PULL-ONLY hard deny names 1-REPORTER. Gate `check_boot_brief` v1.3: B6/B7 re-pointed to the OTV5 brief (born red on v2.2), B9 pins the no-action settings (born red on the r5 settings). FLAGGED, not changed: WA §38 itself still names QQQ-TEST as controller (the brief tells the agent how to read it); `tools/claude_boot.py` defaults the Remote Control name to "✨<SYM>-TEST" when OT_RC_NAME is unset. | ✅ CLOSED r6 |
| **DOC.35** | ✅ **CLOSED r5** — docs/ENV.md WENT STALE THE MOMENT A REVISION MOVED A LINE. r4 shifted lines in tools/boot_sweep.py and tests/check_clean_deploy.py and the generated `docs/ENV.md` (every OT_* read, with file:line) was not regenerated, so `check_env_doc` E1 was red on the next store-less sweep (QQQ-TEST MSG-QQQ-1025-03, 287/288) - the one red the operator's first box would have shown. The lander regenerated the two maps at land and not this third generated document. r5 regenerates ENV.md and `tools/land.sh` v1.24 runs `tests/gen_env_doc.py` after the write map and stages `docs/ENV.md` by name, exactly as the maps; the regeneration runs BEFORE the CHECK stage (so a spec may list `check_env_doc` and pass on the drift it fixes) and FAILS CLOSED (a generator that exits non-zero or writes nothing refuses the land by name). `check_land_spec_parse` v1.2 L12-L14 drive it with stub generators in the scratch repo; born red on v1.23. Critiqued by QQQ-TEST (MSG-QQQ-1025-05: the order, the fail-closed). | ✅ CLOSED r5 |
| **GATE.5** | ✅ **CLOSED r4** — check_clean_deploy C17 WAS RED UNDER THE BOOT SWEEP AND GREEN BARE. `import utils.instrument` at C17 relied on ROOT being on sys.path, which only s3_push.own_symbol()'s bot-unit branch did; with OT_INSTRUMENT set (boot_sweep always sets it) own_symbol returns early and the import failed "No module named 'utils'". A checker defect, not a product one; it would have been a standing red on every OTV5 box's 08:05 sweep and on the first fresh box. Found by QQQ-TEST's sweep of a store-less OTV5 r1 tree (MSG-QQQ-1024-47, 287/288). v1.1 puts ROOT on sys.path itself. Control: `OT_INSTRUMENT=QQQ python3 tests/check_clean_deploy.py` red before, green after. | ✅ CLOSED r4 |
| **GATE.6** | ✅ **CLOSED r4** — THE SWEEP AND THE LANDER LET A CHECKER TOUCH THE LIVE HEARTBEAT. position_manager's mark fallback calls utils.heartbeat.touch, whose file is data/BOT_HEARTBEAT unless OT_HEARTBEAT_FILE moves it; tools/emergency_watchdog.py reads that mtime as the bot's liveness. boot_sweep.run_one scratched eleven paths but not this one (the 10-24 sweep wrote "<epoch> mark fallback" into the live file), and the lander's CHECK stage had the same gap - so a checker refreshed the watchdog's signal; harmless while the bot is inactive, it masks a hung bot for one staleness window. Found by QQQ-TEST (MSG-QQQ-1024-47). boot_sweep v1.9 and land.sh v1.23 hand every checker a scratch OT_HEARTBEAT_FILE; check_boot_sweep v1.8 B3/B3b and check_land_tooling v1.8 T6 require it (born red on boot_sweep v1.8 / land.sh v1.22). The same gap exists on the frozen predecessor; noted, not fixed there. | ✅ CLOSED r4 |
| **DOC.34** | ✅ **CLOSED r3** — THE README'S TRADE TABLE DISAGREED WITH THE ROSTER. README v1.0 said ORCS "ON, paper only" while `config.py` (ORCS_ENABLED off since OTV4TEST r249; `OT_ORCS=1` restores it) and `docs/TRADES.md` §0 say OFF, and it omitted PARK (ON, 11:01-11:03, TRADES §9). Found when the operator asked whether the README is accurate (2026-10-25). v1.1 corrects both rows and states that TRADES §0, pinned to config by `check_docs_roster`, is the roster of record and the README table defers to it. Docs only. | ✅ CLOSED r3 |
| **BOOT.8** | ✅ **CLOSED r2** — THE FIRST-BOOT BRIEF CONTRADICTED THE INSTALL IT BRIEFS. `docs/FIRST_BOOT.md` v1.4 told the fresh box's agent to expect `candle-feed` and `optionsbot` active and enabled and listed "a unit not active or not enabled" as always a defect, while the install (CD.1, `deploy/install_services.sh`) ships both disabled and stopped and the push off until the operator's configure.sh Done. The agent raised at the end of the install (the operator runs it with the Claude session active from the first run, 2026-10-24) would have reported the install's design as two defects and had no way to check the feed or the bot. v1.5: Phase 1 (before Done) states the correct install - both trading services disabled and inactive, the suite enabled, standalone capture with a push guard, the capture request recorded, no first-start marker, no stores, tools/capture_guard.py as the one pre-check that predicts whether Done will start the bot (the primer refuses "feed store not found" before candle-feed exists, by design); Phase 2 (after Done) keeps the warm-up section and the running-box checks. "Start nothing" joins the rules and names deploy/install_candle_feed.sh (it enables and restarts the feed when run by hand). Critiqued by QQQ-TEST (MSG-QQQ-1024-46): optbot-claude-boot is conditional on the bootstrap's OT_CLAUDE_AT_BOOT=1 (default 0), so the brief expects six suite units plus that one when the flag was set; standalone leaves s3-push MASKED with the never-push drop-in and candle-logger / self-close absent or disabled. Gate: check_bootstrap G10/G14 (the pinned strings: READ-ONLY, last_session.py, WARMING UP, BACKFILL_DAYS, 1h 45 days, T7, ListAgents, SendMessage, not the operator) still hold. | ✅ CLOSED r2 |
| **SEED.4** | ✅ **CLOSED r1** — THE PULL-ONLY AGENT BRIEF DESCRIBED A REMOTE THAT DOES NOT EXIST. `deploy/claude-user-settings-pull-only.json` told the box's agent its checkout had an `upstream` remote (another repository's URL) beside `origin`; `setup_ec2.sh` adds only `origin`, so the brief named a remote no install creates. Found by QQQ-TEST's review of the seed (MSG-QQQ-1024-44). The three clauses are gone; `install_claude.sh` renders the file, `check_origin` O4/O5 and `check_boot_brief` B8 read it. | ✅ CLOSED r1 |
| **LAND.2** | ✅ **CLOSED r1** — THE LANDER TOOK THE FIRST CHECKOUT THAT CARRIED THE MARKERS. `tools/land.sh` resolved a half to the first `$HOME/*/` checkout carrying every REPO marker, in glob order; this tree beside the tree it was seeded from, or beside the mainline tree on control, would have captured the other's land silently. v1.22 collects every match, refuses two or more by name, and the spec directive `ORIGIN <owner/repo>` picks the checkout whose git origin ends in it (a lone match disagreeing with a declared ORIGIN is refused). `check_land_spec_parse` v1.1 L6-L8, born red on v1.21 at L6/L7. r0's ledger row claimed this rule for this tree before the lander carried it; r1 makes it true. | ✅ CLOSED r1 |
| **HYG.3** | ✅ **CLOSED r0** — `tests/check_ledger_parity.py` v1.6 reads `docs/GENESIS.md`, which IS this tree's one ledger (the fork's two-ledger arrangement, where the gate read a frozen file, does not exist here); its allow-lists of another ledger's row-less numbers are empty and cross-repo citations are stripped before L3.
